# Seed 36 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - from scratch: the anchoring test (Iteration 123)

`seed 36 tag2 4h nocarry then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6
--frontier --cpus 2,8`, commit 2b11272 (Iteration 114), started 22:36:
**nothing carried in** - the hand-made designs, the founders and their
mutants only - four workers (cpus 2, 8, 12, 14), population 256, 53
generations before the deadline (2 h 40 min of evolution), 13,508 designs;
751 refused by the reach limit, 11 old-format designs that do not convert.
Session 31 measured it with seeds 30, 32, 33 and 35 (calibration 1.007; cpu 2
1.007, cpu 8 1.011) - with --frontier, 362 designs in 63 minutes (660 in 2 h 4
min before): inside the hours' reserve.

**The anchoring test (the owner: does adding genes one at a time skew the
search?): no sign of it.** A search with no carried front climbed fast - its
best speed 0.812 at the start, 0.254 by generation 10, 0.148 by 20, 0.141 at
the end; its smallest 25,369 -> 20,905 by generation 10 -> 20,425 - into the
region the carried runs hold, and not past them:

| objective | seed 36, from scratch | the carried runs, best | |
|---|---|---|---|
| fastest | 0.146 (session 31) | 0.130 (seed 35) | 12% slower |
| smallest binary + image | 20,425 | 20,318 (seed 35) | 0.5% larger |
| least memory | 188 KB | 186 KB | inside the tie |

In every speed band its smallest design is 0.5-6% larger than the carried
runs'. On the front of all runs (90): **2** of seed 36 - 396dad55ea (0.176 at
29,592 bytes) and 1a762b3fa9 (0.207 at 24,868), both in the middle - against
70 of seed 35. And it chose as they did: the tag on all 119 of its front
designs, kfast 115, opbody 92, kinput 86, noinls 86, alignfn 76, t2wide 71,
hotinl 56, the JIT 20. So the carried runs are not anchored away from better
designs that a fresh start finds; a fresh start of 2 h 40 min reaches their
neighbourhood and no further. (A longer one might go further - not tested.)

The stopping rule against seeds 30-35: no improvement on any objective.

## The run's report

# Evolved VM designs

13508 designs evaluated, 12746 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, parse, corpus, loop,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
